Entry 1
Category: Classical Literature
Concept: Epic
Definition: A long narrative poem about heroic deeds.
Key Points:
- Fundamental literary concept.
- Helps in understanding and analysing texts.
Example: Epic is widely discussed in literary studies.
Related Concepts:
Themes, Genres, Authors, Literary Devices

Category: Classical Literature
Concept: Epic
Definition: A long narrative poem about heroic deeds.
Key Points:
- Fundamental literary concept.
- Helps in understanding and analysing texts.
Example: Epic is widely discussed in literary studies.

Themes, Genres, Authors, Literary Devices
